# QA Scoreboard and Repository Health

**Status:** Canonical verification record  
**Last reviewed:** August 29, 2026 (UTC)

This document separates source-derived counts from commands that have actually been run. A QA declaration is counted syntactically; it is considered verified only when its module check passes. QA proves selected consequences relative to Scaffold’s axioms and does not prove those axioms.

| Mathlib cache provenance | Note | 2026-08-21 | The local `lake exe cache` binary crashes under the current macOS dyld (`__DATA_CONST segment missing SG_READ_ONLY flag`); the cache is fetched by running the same Cache tool logic interpreted via `lake env lean --run`, unpacking 5685 Mathlib oleans. Re-applied on 2026-08-18 after the local `.lake/build` state was found pruned (Mathlib oleans down to 1795); the interpreted fetch restored the full set, after which `lake build` recompiled the residue (2008 targets) and passed. Re-applied on 2026-08-19 (pruned state found at run start; fetch restored the full set; the affected module builds then elapsed normally). Re-applied **twice** on 2026-08-20: the pruned state recurred at run start (fetch #1 restored the full set), and two `lake build` invocations interrupted at their tool timeouts left the Mathlib olean dir wiped mid-replay (fetch #2 restored it); the subsequent *uninterrupted* `lake build` replayed the trace residue and passed (2184 targets). Recorded operational rule: never let `lake build` be killed mid-replay — schedule a timeout comfortably above the ~35–50 min residue replay, or elaborate modules directly with `lake env lean`; alternatively run the build detached from the tool timeout (a log file + process poll), which is how the 2026-08-20 Tikhonov run completed its 2185-target build after one invocation was killed at its tool timeout (the Mathlib olean set survived that kill; no re-fetch was needed). Re-applied at the 2026-08-20 band Step-2 run start (pruned state recurred, 0 present; interpreted fetch restored 5387, "Unpacked in 21270 ms"). At the 2026-08-20 band Step-3 run start the same check pattern returned 0 again and a fetch ran ("Unpacked in 22688 ms"), but a later audit found this was a **wrong-path artifact**: the oleans live at `.lake/packages/mathlib/.lake/build/lib/Mathlib/...` with **no `lean/` path level** (consistent with the case-insensitivity note below), so `ls .../build/lib/lean/Mathlib | wc -l` returns 0 even with the full set present — the correct presence check is a `find … -name "*.olean" | wc -l` at the package build root (5387 with the set intact). A `lake build` invocation killed at its 300 s tool timeout during Mathlib trace replay left the set intact (verified by the corrected count), and one redundant re-fetch ran (idempotent, "Unpacked in 2022 ms"); the final full build of the run was executed detached per the recorded procedure. Recorded operational rule (amended 2026-08-20): check olean presence at the correct path before fetching, never let `lake build` be killed mid-replay — schedule a timeout comfortably above the ~35–50 min residue replay, or elaborate modules directly with `lake env lean`; alternatively run the build detached from the tool timeout (a log file + process poll), which is how the 2026-08-20 Tikhonov run completed its 2185-target build after one invocation was killed at its tool timeout (the Mathlib olean set survived that kill; no re-fetch was needed). Invocation note from the Step-2 run: under `lake --dir=.lake/packages/mathlib`, the `--run` file argument resolves relative to the *current* directory, not `--dir` — invoke from the repo root as `lake --dir=.lake/packages/mathlib env lean --run .lake/packages/mathlib/Cache/Main.lean get` (passing `Cache/Main.lean` alone fails with "file not found"). A macOS case-insensitivity trap was also recorded: creating `.lake/build/lib/lean/` in this workspace shadows the toolchain's core `Lean/` tree in `LEAN_PATH` (breaking all elaboration with "object file ... Lean.Elab.Command.olean does not exist"); this repo's olean layout is `.lake/build/lib/Scaffold/...` directly, with no `lean/` level. Environment healthy at the 2026-08-21 Step-4 run start (correct-path check: 5387 present, no fetch needed); the QA-module batch re-run needed one restart at its 10-minute tool timeout mid-batch (no state damage — `lake env lean` does not replay traces), and completed on the second pass; the full build ran detached per the recorded procedure and passed. |

## Interpretation

- **Dissolution completed across the window family — the floor and swept-cut theorems made unconditional (2026-08-29, the dissolution delivery's own priced follow-on; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +5, total 3008).** The bracket's dissolution left the family's other two members (`edgePerturbation_normalized_cheeger_floor`, `edgePerturbation_fiedler_sweep_cut_tail`) still carrying their `perturbAdmissible` conjuncts; this delivery applies the identical decomposition to both, so every measured event in the window family — floor, bracket, and the algorithm-facing swept-cut capstone — now reads with **no conditioning conjunct**, at the honest price of one degree-tail term. The falsifiability content sits in the **strict-containment witness** (hard crust): the all-false outcome is provably *in* the unconditional sweep bad event (its empty resampled graph is disconnected, by the kernel-constancy contrapositive at the zero adjacency) while provably *not* admissible — so the dissolution genuinely enlarged the measured event, and the degree-tail term is the honest price of exactly those outcomes; a decomposition that quietly shrank the event would break this witness. Beside it, the good-outcome conjunction (connected with a swept cut at `conductance² ≤ 4` inside the ceiling `81/4` — the bad event is not all of `Ω`) and the two closed-form instances (`4 exp(−1/4096) + 4 exp(−1/16)` each) pin the constants end-to-end. Both new theorems are two-axiom members (`matrix_hoeffding` + `hoeffding_inequality`, each via its own sub-theorem, `#print axioms`-verified) and must never be described as foundationally proved; the sweep member's floor-positivity guard remains proof-load-bearing exactly as in the conditional capstone (the C₄ dropped-guard refutation fixture carries over verbatim).
- **Admissibility dissolution delivered — the window family's first unconditional measured event (2026-08-29, `proposals/hoeffding-inequality-degree-concentration.md`'s priced follow-on; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +16, total 3003).** Every measured event of the irregular window family had carried the `perturbAdmissible` conjunct, recorded honestly as "proof-load-bearing, not fixture-refuted"; this delivery *derives* the conjunct — nonnegativity from the pair design condition `p e + p eᵀ ≤ 1` (a new public engine lemma, tight at the `p ≡ ½` boundary where the pinned all-false entry is exactly `0`, and *refuted when dropped* at `p ≡ 9/10` where the entry is `−4/5`), the degree window from a shrunk base window plus the delivered degree tails (the first composition of the degree-tail family with the window family, consummating the degree-tail delivery's stated purpose: "the first step toward deriving admissibility rather than hypothesizing it"). The falsifiability content: the engine's inequality is pinned tight at the boundary outcome; the dropped-pair-condition fence is a genuine negative witness at a legal `p ∈ [0,1]` design; and the strictness of the transfer's `< s` is pinned coherent (the boundary outcome sits in the degree event and is provably inadmissible). The resulting theorem is the family's first — and so far only — deliberate two-axiom member (`matrix_hoeffding` and `hoeffding_inequality` together, each via its own sub-theorem, `#print axioms`-verified); it must never be described as foundationally proved.
- **Bernstein twin delivered — the variance-adaptive degree tails make two more zero-consumer axioms load-bearing (2026-08-29, `proposals/hoeffding-inequality-degree-concentration.md`'s priced follow-on; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +10, total 2987).** After the degree-concentration delivery, three admitted axioms remained with zero theorem consumers (`bernstein_inequality`, `bernstein_bounded_variance`, `hoeffding_lemma`); this delivery gives the first two graph-theoretic consumers in one slice, at the same centered Bernoulli edge-resampling design, with only one new piece of engine content (the second-moment identity `∫ (δ_e − p_e)² = p e (1 − p e)`). The falsifiability content: the true variance statistic is pinned exactly (`σ²₀ = ½` on K₂ at the fair coin) with the Poisson-trial degeneration (`∑ w² p = 1`) refuted — a future edit dropping the `(1 − p)` factor from the design's variance lemma breaks that refutation; the fourfold variance reduction is an equation (`σ²₀ = S₀/4`, sharp at the fair coin); and the **strict variance-adaptivity improvement is itself a proved theorem** (`2 exp(−3/5) < 2 exp(−1/4)`), the cross-axiom coherence check showing the Bernstein tail genuinely stronger than the delivered Hoeffding tail at moderate thresholds, not a reparametrization. The two Derived tails are conditional on their own axiom alone (`#print axioms`-verified, zero cross-contact) and say so; QA does not prove either axiom. Only `hoeffding_lemma` remains zero-consumer — its natural consumer is `bernstein_inequality`'s own proof, upstream work.

- **`hoeffding_inequality`'s first theorem consumer delivered — vertex-degree concentration under edge resampling, the window family's missing complementary concentration (2026-08-29, `proposals/hoeffding-inequality-degree-concentration.md`; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +14, total 2977).** After the 2026-08-28 integrability audit, four admitted axioms still had zero theorem consumers (`hoeffding_inequality`, `bernstein_inequality`, `bernstein_bounded_variance`, `hoeffding_lemma`) — audited safe but never load-bearing, so a misstated clause in any of them would break nothing. This delivery makes the first one carry weight exactly where the shelf needs it: the irregular window family's honesty notes record that the λ₂/norm tails carry zero degree information (`L(E_ω)·1 = 0` identically), which is why `perturbAdmissible`'s degree clauses are event-internal hypotheses; the per-vertex degree tail `μ {|deg G_ω v − deg A v| ≥ t} ≤ 2 exp(−t²/(2 S_v))` is that missing half, and the all-vertices union bound is the first step toward deriving admissibility rather than hypothesizing it. The falsifiability content: the exact event measure `1/2` is computed through the design's own independence machinery (`indepFun_coord` + `toMeasure_cyl`), independently of the tail theorem, so the bound's fixture-scale slack is on the record; the variance statistic's ordered-pair double count is pinned with the single-counted value refuted; and the deviation identity holds by two independent routes at the all-true outcome (raw weight-space arithmetic vs the design's own identity — a wrong entry formula breaks exactly one). The two Derived tails are conditional on `hoeffding_inequality` alone and say so; QA does not prove the axiom.

- **Degenerate-degree corner audit delivered — the parked junk-spectrum finding settled in proved form, and the two attacked honesty notes stand (2026-08-29, `proposals/matrix-hoeffding-spectral-gap-estimation.md`'s corner-audit follow-on record; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +12, total 2963).** Two things distinguish this delivery from a documentation pass. First, the run *attacked* the shelf's two named dropped-guard targets before selecting, and both honesty notes **survived with structural reasons now on record**: the sharpened drift's `s = γ` corner because the bound's variance proxy is the *deterministic* `‖∑ L_e²‖` (p-independent — the small-`p` collapse route fails) and the exponent is weight-scale-invariant (capped ≈ `2/3` on hand fixtures against the needed `ln(2d)` — the heavy-edge route fails); the window family's dropped admissibility because a resampled adjacency with at most one positive eigendirection forces `λ₂(L_sym) ≥ 1 > floor` by congruence inertia. Second, those analyses consumed the parked junk-spectrum finding three times over — which is exactly the pricing its "if one is ever priced" gate asked for. The settled fact: at nonpositive degrees (`√0 = 0` with `0⁻¹ = 0` on the zero side, `Real.sqrt` of a negative on the other — the hitherto-unrecorded *negative-degree* corner, the `p ≠ ½` outcomes route) the normalized Laplacian degenerates to the identity whose sorted spectrum is `1` at every index, and the QA exhibits the easy mistake as a proved contrast — the same all-false outcome carrying the zero matrix's `λ₂(L) = 0` and the identity's `λ₂(L_sym) = 1` side by side — with the honesty note's floor-condition mechanism (the un-windowed floor condition false at the fixture) proved rather than asserted. All fifteen audited declarations at the standard three; nothing conditional anywhere in the audit.

- **C₄ dropped-guard refutation of the sweep-cut tail's floor-positivity guard delivered — the honesty note corrected on the record (2026-08-29, `proposals/matrix-hoeffding-spectral-gap-estimation.md`'s C₄ guard-fence record; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +29, total 2951).** The previous run recorded the guard as "proof-load-bearing with no fixture-scale refutation (the tail bound exceeds `1` at 2–3 vertices)"; that obstruction analysis was anchored on the eigenvalue-floor membership route — which is t-monotone and evasive — and on fixtures where no admissible-but-disconnected outcome exists at all. The delivered fence exhibits both gaps at once: on C₄ at the hypothesis-loosened window `[1, 2]`, the perfect-matching outcome is admissible (degrees all `2`) and disconnected, its bad-set membership via `¬connected` is `t`-invariant, and at `t = 9000` the collapsed bound `8/(1 + 9000²/128)` sits strictly below its exact atom mass `1/65536` — the un-guarded statement *false in proved arithmetic*, with `#print axioms` at the standard three (a refutation cannot consume the axiom-conditional theorem). The fence is load-bearing on the design's exact shapes (`perturbWeight`'s off-diagonal formula, `perturbAdmissible`'s degree window, `bernPMF`'s atom masses) and on the connectivity⇒kernel-constants theorem; the delivery also exhibits the admissibility window doing real exclusion work beside the guard (at the tight window `[2,2]` every admissible C₄ outcome keeps the full cycle and the fixture disappears).**

- **Swept-Fiedler-cut consumer of the normalized window delivered — the window family's algorithm-facing capstone (2026-08-29, `proposals/matrix-hoeffding-spectral-gap-estimation.md`'s sweep-cut follow-on record; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +4, total 2922).** The chain concentration → eigenvalue window → connectivity → *explicit algorithmic output* is now closed under one statement: with high probability, every admissible resampling outcome is connected and its spectral-partitioning sweep returns a certified cut at `conductance² ≤ 2·(2·dmax·φ + t)/dmin`. Three delivered families are load-bearing simultaneously — the degree sandwich (through the bracket), the connectivity transfer (the floor's positivity is *exactly* its `0 < λ₂` input, recorded as the new hypothesis's proof-obligation), and the volume-weighted sweep extraction (the ceiling caps its `2λ₂` bound) — and the QA good-outcome witness joins the deterministic sweep theorem to the pinned all-true spectrum (`conductance² ≤ 4` against the window's `16.25`). The theorem is conditional on `matrix_hoeffding` and must never be described as foundationally proved.
- **Irregular (normalized) Cheeger window delivered — the degree sandwich's consumer, closing the loop the same day it opened (2026-08-29, `proposals/matrix-hoeffding-spectral-gap-estimation.md`'s follow-on record; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +16, total 2918).** The window family now has both spellings: the regular combinatorial window (delivered 2026-08-28) and the irregular normalized one, the latter on arbitrary symmetric nonnegative positive-degree base graphs with no regularity. The load-bearing structure is the three-way composition — an error in the degree sandwich's interfaces, in the new window-Cheeger engine constants, or in the delivered λ₂ tails breaks the corresponding half of the bracket's `measure_mono` — and the QA pins the engine ceiling at equality (`λ₂ = 2 = 2·(1·φ)`), the genuinely irregular `P₃` instances, the scale-invariance mechanism, and both sides of the admissibility window doing real exclusion work (the all-false outcome by the degree floor, the `P₃` all-true by the degree ceiling). Recorded honestly: the admissibility window is *proof*-load-bearing but admits no dropped-window refutation fixture at fixture scale — the normalized Laplacian's junk value at zero-degree corners is the *identity's* spectrum (`λ₂ = 1`), not the zero matrix's, which keeps the un-windowed floor condition false on `K₂`-shaped fixtures; the spike-level junk-value finding is recorded for the next corner audit. The two Derived theorems are conditional on `matrix_hoeffding` and must never be described as foundationally proved.
- **Degree eigenvalue sandwich delivered — the eigenvalue-level bridge between the combinatorial and normalized worlds (2026-08-29, `proposals/degree-eigenvalue-sandwich.md`; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +37, total 2902).** The irregular Cheeger pair and the combinatorial λ₂ tails previously shared no spectrum-level interface; the sandwich `λₖ(L)/dmax ≤ λₖ(L_sym) ≤ λₖ(L)/dmin` at every sorted index is that interface, at exactly the irregular pair's own hypothesis shape (symmetric nonnegative positive degrees, no connectivity). The falsifiability content is structural: pre-delivery analysis established that no pointwise test-vector route can prove either side (the two variational constraint sets `x ⊥ 1` and `x ⊥ √D·1` mismatch under the degree substitution), so the theorem is provable only through the subspace Courant–Fischer machinery — an error in the congruence `√D L_sym √D = L`, in `laplacian_psd`, or in the CF witness forms breaks the proof outright. QA pins both endpoints the way the constants deserve: the P₃ upper side is attained at *equality* (`λ₂(L_sym) = 1 = λ₂/dmin`), the K₂ regular squeeze is tight on *both* ends (`2 ≤ 2 ≤ 2`), the `dmin`/`dmax` pairing is fenced against swapping (`1 ≤ 1/2` refuted), the engine is exercised at a non-second index through independent trace-route pins, and the `0 < dmin` guard is fenced by a genuine junk-instantiation refutation (on `K₂ ⊕` an isolated vertex the degree-floor hypothesis still holds at `dmin = 0` while the un-guarded upper side reads `1 ≤ lambda2/0 = 0`). The irregular Cheeger window assembly — composing the λ₂ tails with this bridge and the irregular pair — is the recorded open follow-on. |
- **Sharpened drift interface delivered — the matched-threshold `s/(γ−s)` pair for the edge-resampling design (2026-08-29, the pipeline's priced follow-on; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +10, total 2865).** The delivered drift pair bounds `μ{‖rotation‖ ≥ t/δ}` against a tail at level `t` — at a fixed threshold the exponent is then not envelope-optimal, and `eventStreamProjectorDrift`'s statement shape (threshold `s/(γ−s)` matched to the tail level `s`) is the form consumers actually quote. The sharpened pair closes that interface gap by consuming the gap parameter inline (`γ ≤ λ₃ − λ₂` replaces the hand split `t + δ ≤ gap`), and the QA pins that this is a real strengthening rather than a reparametrization: the envelope arithmetic is *proved* (every valid `(t, δ)` instance is dominated at the same threshold — `t ≤ γt/(t+δ)` with `(γt/(t+δ))/(γ−γt/(t+δ)) = t/δ` — so the sharpened family is exactly the envelope of the delivered one), and the concrete pins exhibit the strictness numerically (at threshold `2` on the path: sharpened `6 exp(−2/27)` vs the valid naive `(1, ½)` instance's `6 exp(−1/24)`, with `6 exp(−2/27) < 6 exp(−1/24)` proved by strict exp monotonicity). A wrong constant anywhere on the sharpened parametrization breaks the `6 exp(−2/27)` closed form at `s = 4/3`. The two sharpened theorems are conditional on `matrix_hoeffding` alone and say so; QA does not prove the axiom.
- **Cheeger-window consumer of the λ₂ tail delivered — the high-probability connectivity window under random edge resampling (2026-08-28, `proposals/matrix-hoeffding-spectral-gap-estimation.md`, the Cheeger-window follow-on record; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +12, total 2855).** The concentration bridge's newest consumer is the Cheeger center itself: on a `d`-regular base graph, the resampled graph's algebraic connectivity stays inside the window `[d·φ²/2 − t, 2dφ + t]` outside a set of the λ₂ tail's measure. The load-bearing content is structural, not just instantial: *both* Cheeger directions are consumed by the inclusion (the floor side through `cheeger_lower_bound_laplacian`, the ceiling side through `cheeger_upper_bound_laplacian` — the new public-API engine pair transporting the proved Cheeger pair to the combinatorial `lambda2` spelling via `L = d • L_sym`), so a wrong constant on either side breaks the corresponding half of the `measure_mono` — the window is not a restatement of the tail but a genuinely Cheeger-driven narrowing of it. QA makes both sides falsifiable on the design: the ceiling attained with equality on `K₂` (`λ₂ = 2 = 2·(1·φ)`), and both window sides witnessed at concrete outcomes (all-false below the floor with `λ₂ = 0` attained at equality — random resampling really can destroy the certificate, which is exactly what the tail prices; all-true above the ceiling at `λ₂ = 4 ≥ 5/2`). The two Derived theorems are conditional on `matrix_hoeffding` alone and say so; QA does not prove the axiom.
- **Eigenvalue-level edge-perturbation tail delivered — spectral-gap concentration, the proposal's namesake packaging (2026-08-28, `proposals/matrix-hoeffding-spectral-gap-estimation.md`; zero new axioms, count stays 10; QA +13, total 2843).** The delivered tail landed in operator-norm language; the SGT center's currency is `evals`. The join with the *proved* Weyl inequality moves the whole statement into spectrum language — every Laplacian eigenvalue of the randomly resampled graph concentrates around the base graph's at the matrix-Hoeffding rate, with the one-sided form reading as "random resampling does not destroy the spectral gap" and the λ₂ spelling landing directly at the Fiedler/Cheeger interface. The falsifiability content is the tightness pin: at a genuine design outcome (the all-true outcome on K₂, where the resampled graph is exactly the weight-2 edge) the Weyl transfer is an *equality* — `|4 − 2| = ‖L(E_ω)‖ = 2` with the two sides computed by independent routes (kernel-plus-trace spectra vs the packaging pin joined to the two-sided rank-one norm), so a constant mistake anywhere on the transfer path breaks a proved theorem. The uniform-form packaging's `x ≠ 0` guard gets the same treatment: the un-guarded event is provably all of `Ω` with measure `1`, refuting the un-guarded bound in proved arithmetic — the guard is mathematics, not decoration. - **Explicit axiom:** an intentional trust boundary declared with Lean's `axiom` command. QA lemmas and derived theorems using these axioms are conditional on them.
- **Admitted proof:** a `sorry` or `admit` accepted by Lean; this is different from an explicit axiom and remains technical debt in public modules. As of this scoreboard there are no `sorry`/`admit` tokens anywhere under `Scaffold/`.
- **QA declaration:** a theorem or lemma under `Scaffold/QA`; all 1243 declarations have compiled against the current public API.
- **Default build:** the umbrella reaches every public module listed above; no public module is excluded from `lake build`.
- **Decidable spectral certificates, step 2 — the Expander Mixing Lemma (2026-08-19):** `proposals/decidable-spectral-certificates.md` (High) at its named next step, delivered as pure hard crust in `GraphTheory.Expander` plus two generic additions to `GraphTheory.Spectral` — **zero new axioms** (13 unchanged). **The theorem:** for a symmetric, nonnegative, `d`-regular network with Laplacian-spectrum hypothesis `μ ≥ max |d − λ₂(L)| |d − λ_max(L)|`, the `(S,T)` cut weight deviates from the population main term `d•|S|•|T|/|V|` by at most `μ • √(|S||T|(|V|−|S|)(|V|−|T|))/|V|` — the classical discrepancy bridge between algebraic spectral gaps and combinatorial pseudorandomness, at the Step 0 restated `√` signature. **The route is cheaper and stronger than the sketched eigenspace expansion:** a Rayleigh sandwich on `1⊥` — the `d`-regular identity `A + L = D` in quadratic-form form trading the proved lower bound `λ₂•‖x‖² ≤ xᵀLx` (the center's `secondEval_le_rayleigh` multiplied out) against the new generic top bound `xᵀLx ≤ λ_max•‖x‖²` (`quadForm_le_evals_last`, supported by `eigvalOf_le_evals_last`) — giving the operator bound `|xᵀAx| ≤ μ‖x‖²` on `1⊥`, then the sharp bilinear form `(x ⬝ᵥ (A *ᵥ y))² ≤ μ²‖x‖²‖y‖²` by the scaling trick (`√Y•x ± √X•y` through the polarization identity; the choice `a² = Y, b² = X` attains the AM–GM equality — no slack), then the variance identity `‖centeredIndicator S‖² = |S|(|V|−|S|)/|V|` evaluating the geometric factor. No eigenspace split of `A`, no kernel case analysis, no affine spectrum transfer. **Statement-shape strengthening recorded:** the Step 0 sketch's `hloop` hypothesis is dropped — the `A + L = D` identity needs regularity only. The specialization is load-bearing on the Woodbury repair — a residual defect there would surface in this proof rather than pass beside it. QA (`Core/MatrixUpdates_QA.lean`, +4 declarations): the rank-one positive instance at the existing `A2 = diag 2 2` fixture — `A + u vᵀ = !![3,1;1,3]` — with the left side computed independently by the adjugate formula (`sherman_morrison_sum_inv_QA`) and the theorem-consumed right side pinned to the same `!![3/8,-1/8;-1/8,3/8]` (`sherman_morrison_rhs_QA`, denominator computed to `1` from the definitions); and the negative witness at the excluded denominator — `A = !![1]`, `u = ![2]`, `v = ![-1/2]` gives `v ⬝ᵥ (A⁻¹ *ᵥ u) = -1` exactly (`sherman_morrison_excluded_denominator_QA`) and the update `!![1] + !![-1] = !![0]` is singular there (`sherman_morrison_singular_witness_QA`) — the hypothesis excludes exactly the vacuous-junk point.

It is now a theorem at the same name with the standard middle factor `(C⁻¹ + V A⁻¹ U)⁻¹` and exactly the three explicit hypotheses `IsUnit A.det`, `IsUnit C.det`, `IsUnit (C⁻¹ + V A⁻¹ U).det` (the former `IsUnit (A + U C V).det` hypothesis is derivable and dropped — Mathlib's `Matrix.invertibleAddMulMul` constructs the sum's `Invertible` instance). - **`lambda2_variational` retirement (2026-08-18):** the Courant–Fischer characterization of the algebraic connectivity was converted from an admitted axiom (symmetry hypotheses only — a materially false shape for negative weights, refuted in QA) into a proved theorem with the load-bearing `hnonneg` hypothesis, reducing the explicit axiom count from 18 to 17. The proof is pure matrix-world hard crust (eigenbasis expansion, Parseval, spectral resolution of `quadForm`, and two sorted-multiset multiplicity pins), consuming no axioms.
- **Azuma statement repair (2026-08-17, same day as the axiom's introduction):** preparing the derivation exposed that `matrix_azuma_hoeffding` lacked the summand-count factor `m` in the exponent denominator, which made the statement false for `m ≥ 2` (Rademacher-sum counterexample). The denominator is now `8 m R²`, the uniform-bound specialization of Tropp's variance statistic `σ² = ‖∑ Yₖ²‖ ≤ m R²`. The axiom had no downstream consumers before the repair.
